# Router Benchmark

## Environment

- Date: 2026-05-22
- Git commit: 4e7f78c732b216d0dc088186ae54065680c2dcd6
- Node: v24.15.0
- NODE_ENV: production
- pnpm: 10.19.0
- Platform: linux x64
- CPU: Intel(R) Xeon(R) Platinum 8370C CPU @ 2.80GHz (4)
- Memory: 16765390848 bytes
- Package versions:
  - @builder.io/qwik: 1.19.2
  - @qwik.dev/core: 2.0.0-beta.35
  - @qwik.dev/router: 2.0.0-beta.35
  - marko: 5.38.39
  - next: 16.2.6
  - react: 19.2.6
  - react-dom: 19.2.6
  - solid-js: 1.9.12
  - solid-js-2: 2.0.0-beta.13

## Rankings

### app render 1000 nodes

Renders a production app route that emits 1,000 simple text spans.

| rank | framework | case | value | diff vs 1st | unit |
| ---: | --- | --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| 1 | mreact-app-router | app render 1000 nodes | 1421 | best | ops/sec |
| 2 | mreact-app-router+log enabled | app render 1000 nodes | 1411 | -0.7% | ops/sec |
| 3 | marko-run | app render 1000 nodes | 873 | -38.56% | ops/sec |
| 4 | tanstack-start | app render 1000 nodes | 810 | -43% | ops/sec |
| 5 | qwik-city | app render 1000 nodes | 529 | -62.77% | ops/sec |
| 6 | solid-start | app render 1000 nodes | 443 | -68.82% | ops/sec |
| 7 | qwik-router-v2 | app render 1000 nodes | 396 | -72.13% | ops/sec |
| 8 | tanstack-start-solid | app render 1000 nodes | 345 | -75.72% | ops/sec |
| 9 | next-app-router | app render 1000 nodes | 87 | -93.88% | ops/sec |
